Bash Category breakdown
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
from collections import Counter
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
cats = Counter()
rows = []
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None: continue
    cats[cat] += 1
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))
print("TOTAL ITEMS:", len(rows))
print("\nCATEGORIES:")
for c, n in cats.most_common():
    print(f"  {n:5d}  {c}")
EOF

Bash Find bars
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows = []
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def search(keywords=None, cats=None, brand_kw=None, max_price=None, min_price=None, limit=30):
    out = []
    for r in rows:
        pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = r
        if cost is None: continue
        d = (desc or "").upper()
        b = (brand or "").upper()
        c = (cat or "").upper()
        if cats and not any(k in c for k in cats): continue
        if keywords and not any(k in d for k in keywords): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_price and cost > max_price: continue
        if min_price and cost < min_price: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x: x[5])
    return out[:limit]

def show(title, results):
    print(f"\n--- {title} ({len(results)}) ---")
    for pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost in results:
        print(f"  ${cost:6.2f}  [{pack:>14}]  {desc}  | {brand[:30]}")

# Granola/snack bars
show("KIND / CLIF / RXBAR / LARABAR / NATURE'S BAKERY BARS",
     search(brand_kw=['KIND','CLIF','RXBAR','LARABAR',"NATURE'S BAKERY","NATURE VALLEY","KASHI","BOBO"], cats=['BARS,CEREAL','BARS, NUTRITIONAL'], limit=40))
EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows = []
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=25, exclude_kw=None):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if exclude_kw and any(k in d for k in exclude_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:46]:46}| {b[:24]}")
    return out

show("TOILET PAPER", desc_kw=['BATH TISSUE','TOILET','TP,'], cats_kw=['PAPER','MISC. NONFOODS','HOUSE'], limit=15)
show("PAPER TOWELS", desc_kw=['PAPR TWL','PAPER TOWEL','PPR TOWEL','PAPR TWLS','PAPR T'], limit=15)
show("FACIAL TISSUE / KLEENEX", desc_kw=['FACIAL TIS','TISSUE','KLEENEX'], cats_kw=['PAPER'], limit=10)
show("LAUNDRY DETERGENT", desc_kw=['LAUNDRY','LNDRY'], cats_kw=['HOUSEHOLD','MISC'], limit=15)
show("DISH SOAP", desc_kw=['DISH'], cats_kw=['HOUSEHOLD'], limit=15)
show("ALL-PURPOSE / CLEANERS", desc_kw=['ALL PURP','MULTI SURF','SURFACE','CLEANER'], cats_kw=['HOUSE'], limit=15)
EOF

Bash Personal care basics
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=25, exclude_kw=None):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if exclude_kw and any(k in d for k in exclude_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:46]:46}| {b[:24]}")
    return out

show("SHAMPOO singles", cats_kw=['HAIR'], desc_kw=['SHAMPOO'], limit=20, max_p=20)
show("CONDITIONER singles", cats_kw=['HAIR'], desc_kw=['COND'], limit=20, max_p=20)
show("BODY WASH / BAR SOAP", cats_kw=['SOAPS, BODY'], limit=20, max_p=15)
show("DEODORANT", cats_kw=['DEODOR'], limit=20, max_p=15)
show("TOOTHPASTE", desc_kw=['TOOTHPASTE','TP,'], cats_kw=['ORAL'], limit=15)
show("FLOSS / TOOTHBRUSH / MOUTHWASH", desc_kw=['FLOSS','BRUSH','MOUTHWSH','MOUTHWASH','RINSE'], cats_kw=['ORAL'], limit=15)
show("FEMININE PADS / TAMPONS", cats_kw=['FEMININE'], limit=20)
show("FACE WASH / CLEANSER", cats_kw=['FACIAL CARE'], desc_kw=['CLEANSER','WASH','CLNS','FACE WASH'], limit=15)
show("MOISTURIZER LOTION", cats_kw=['LOTION'], limit=15, max_p=20)
show("LIP BALM / CHAPSTICK", desc_kw=['LIP BLM','LIP BALM','LIP CARE','CHAPSTICK','LIPBLM'], limit=15)
EOF

Bash Pantry staples search
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=25, exclude_kw=None):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if exclude_kw and any(k in d for k in exclude_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:46]:46}| {b[:24]}")

show("COFFEE GROUND/WHOLE BEAN (under $30)", cats_kw=['COFFEE'], desc_kw=['COFFEE','GRND','BEAN','WHL BN','GROUND'], max_p=30, limit=25)
show("INSTANT COFFEE", desc_kw=['INSTANT'], cats_kw=['COFFEE'], limit=15)
show("TEA BAGS - GREEN/BLACK/HERBAL", cats_kw=['TEA-BAGS'], desc_kw=['GRN','GREEN','BLACK','CHAI','EARL','MINT','CHAM','ROOIBOS'], limit=20)
show("OLIVE OIL", desc_kw=['OLIVE OIL','OLV OIL','EVOO'], cats_kw=['OILS, FOOD'], limit=15)
show("PEANUT BUTTER", desc_kw=['PEANUT BTR','PNT BTR','PEANUT BUT','PNUT BUTTER'], limit=15)
show("OATMEAL / OATS", desc_kw=['OATMEAL','OATS','ROLLED OAT','STL CUT'], limit=20)
show("PASTA - long noodle", cats_kw=['PASTA'], desc_kw=['SPAGHETTI','LINGUINE','FETTUC','PENNE','RIGATONI','ROTINI','MACARONI'], max_p=20, limit=20)
show("PASTA SAUCE", cats_kw=['PASTA SAUCES'], max_p=35, limit=15)
show("RICE", cats_kw=['BEANS GRAINS'], desc_kw=['RICE','JASMINE','BASMATI'], limit=20)
show("CANNED BEANS - chickpea, black", cats_kw=['CANNED'], desc_kw=['CHICKPEA','BLK BEAN','BLACK BEAN','BLK BN','KIDNEY','PNT BN','GARBANZO'], max_p=30, limit=15)
show("TORTILLA CHIPS", desc_kw=['TORTILLA CH','TRTLLA CH','TRTL CHP','TORT CHIP'], max_p=40, limit=15)
show("HUMMUS / DIPS (shelf stable)", desc_kw=['HUMMUS'], limit=10)
show("SALSA - shelf stable", cats_kw=['SALSA','SHELF STABLE SALSA'], limit=15)
show("CRACKERS", cats_kw=['CRACKERS'], max_p=35, limit=15)
show("CEREAL", cats_kw=['CEREAL,PKGD'], max_p=35, limit=20)
show("GRANOLA", desc_kw=['GRANOLA','GRNLA'], max_p=35, limit=15)
EOF

Bash Beverages, frozen, snacks search
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=25, exclude_kw=None):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if exclude_kw and any(k in d for k in exclude_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:46]:46}| {b[:24]}")

show("SPARKLING WATER", cats_kw=['BEVERAGE, WATER','BEVERAGE,CARBONATED'], desc_kw=['SPRKLNG','SPARKLING','LIME','LACROIX','BUBBLY','SPNDRFT','SPINDRIFT'], max_p=30, limit=25)
show("ICED TEA / RTD TEA", cats_kw=['BEVERAGE,TEA'], desc_kw=['BTL','BOTTLE','GLASS','RTD','CHILLED','FZ'], max_p=30, limit=15)
show("COMBUCHA / OLIPOP / POPPI", brand_kw=['OLIPOP','POPPI','GT','HEALTH-ADE','REMEDY'], limit=15, max_p=40)
show("DRINK MIX POWDER / LIQUID IV / ELECTROLYTE", desc_kw=['ELECTROLYT','HYDRATION','LIQUID IV','LMNTE','LMNT'], limit=15, max_p=40)
show("FROZEN PIZZA - cheaper", cats_kw=['FZ LUNCH'], desc_kw=['PIZZA'], max_p=70, limit=15)
show("FROZEN BURRITO / WRAPS", cats_kw=['FZ LUNCH'], desc_kw=['BURRITO','WRAP','BOWL','POCKET'], max_p=70, limit=15)
show("ICE CREAM / FROZEN DESSERTS", cats_kw=['FROZEN DESSERTS'], max_p=50, limit=20)
show("PROTEIN POWDER", desc_kw=['PROTEIN POW','PROTN POW','PRTN POW','WHEY','PROTEIN PWDR'], max_p=70, limit=15)
show("EGGS", cats_kw=['EGGS'], limit=10)
show("MILK shelf-stable", cats_kw=['BEVERAGE,NON-DAIRY','MILK','SHELF STABLE NON-DAIRY'], desc_kw=['ALMOND','OAT','SOY','COCNUT','COCONUT','RICE MILK'], max_p=40, limit=15)
show("PROTEIN BARS - QUEST, RXBAR, BUILT, BARE", brand_kw=['QUEST','RXBAR','BUILT','BARE','PERFECT'], cats_kw=['BARS, NUTRITIONAL','BARS,CEREAL'], max_p=30, limit=15)
show("NUTS / TRAIL MIX", cats_kw=['NUTS,'], max_p=40, limit=15)
show("DARK CHOCOLATE BARS", cats_kw=['CANDY BARS'], desc_kw=['DARK CHOC','DK CHOC','CHOC BAR','CHOCOLATE'], max_p=30, limit=20)
show("POPCORN", desc_kw=['POPCORN','POPCRN','PCRN','PCN '], cats_kw=['CHIPS PRETZELS'], max_p=30, limit=15)
EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=25, exclude_kw=None):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if exclude_kw and any(k in d for k in exclude_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:48]:48}| {b[:24]}")

show("MARINARA / TOMATO SAUCE - jar", cats_kw=['PASTA SAUCES'], desc_kw=['MARINARA','TOMATO BSL','TMTO BAS','TOMATO SAUC','PASTA SAUCE'], max_p=40, limit=20)
show("RAO'S sauce", brand_kw=['RAO','CAMPBELL SALES'], cats_kw=['PASTA SAUCES'], limit=15)
show("BIG PASTA - 16 oz boxes", cats_kw=['PASTA, PACKAGED'], desc_kw=['16 OZ','12 OZ'], max_p=20, limit=20)
show("KETCHUP / MAYO / MUSTARD", cats_kw=['CONDIMENTS'], desc_kw=['KETCHUP','KTSP','MAYO','MUSTARD','SRIRACHA'], max_p=30, limit=15)
show("SOY SAUCE / TAMARI", cats_kw=['CONDIMENTS','TAMARIS'], desc_kw=['SOY','TAMARI'], max_p=30, limit=10)
show("HONEY / MAPLE / AGAVE", cats_kw=['SWEETNRS'], desc_kw=['HONEY','MAPLE','AGAVE'], max_p=40, limit=15)
show("FROZEN VEGGIES / FRUIT", cats_kw=['FROZEN FRUITS'], max_p=40, limit=15)
show("ICE CREAM PINTS - real", cats_kw=['FROZEN DESSERTS'], desc_kw=['PINT','OG2','BEN','HALO'], max_p=70, limit=15)
show("BREAD - shelf stable", cats_kw=['BREAD','FZ BREAD'], desc_kw=['BREAD','LOAF','BAGUET','SOURDOUGH','TORTILLA'], max_p=40, limit=15)
show("CHIPS - regular potato/veggie", cats_kw=['CHIPS PRETZELS'], desc_kw=['POTATO CHIP','POT CHIP','PRTL','PRETZEL'], max_p=30, limit=20)
show("KIND BARS 12-ct cases", brand_kw=['KIND LLC'], cats_kw=['BARS'], limit=20)
show("RXBAR / LARABAR pure", brand_kw=['RXBAR','LARABAR','RX BAR','RAW REV'], limit=15)
show("Tom's toothpaste 6-pack only", brand_kw=["TOM'S"], desc_kw=['6/'], cats_kw=['ORAL'], limit=10)
show("Method/Mrs Meyers Hand Soap", desc_kw=['HAND SOAP','HND SP'], cats_kw=['SOAPS','HOUSE'], max_p=30, limit=15)
show("SUNBUTTER / NUT BUTTER bigger", cats_kw=['SPREADS:NUT'], desc_kw=['JAR','LB','16 OZ','16OZ','BUTTR','BTR'], max_p=40, limit=20)
show("JAM / JELLY / PRESERVES", desc_kw=['JAM','JELLY','PRSV','PRESERVE','MARMALADE','SPREAD'], cats_kw=['FRUIT;'], max_p=30, limit=15)
show("BEEF JERKY / MEAT SNACK", desc_kw=['JERKY','MEAT STICK','BEEF STK'], max_p=40, limit=15)
EOF

Bash Search for fresh produce / tomatoes
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=25):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:50]:50}| {b[:24]} | {c[:25]}")

# Fresh produce search
show("FRESH TOMATOES", desc_kw=['TOMATO','TOMATE'], limit=40, max_p=40)
print("\n--- All unique categories with 'PRODUCE','REFRIGER','FRESH','RF ' ---")
cats=set()
for r in rows:
    c = (r[3] or "").upper()
    if any(k in c for k in ['PRODUCE','RF ','REFRIG']):
        cats.add(c)
for c in sorted(cats):
    print("  ", c)
EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

# Top natural skincare brands to look for
natural_brands = ['WELEDA','BURT','HONEST','EO PROD','DR.BRONNER','DR. BRONNER',
                  'JASON','AVALON','ALBA','ACURE','PACIFICA','MYCHELLE','ANDALOU',
                  'EVAN HEALY','DERMA E','SHIKAI','JOHN MASTER','REN','TATA','OLEHENRIKSEN',
                  'KIEHL','FACE THEORY','BIOSSANCE','PAI','LUMINARY','BETTER PLANET',
                  'A LA MAISON','ATTITUDE','EVERYONE','SUKIN']

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:48]:48}| {b[:28]}")

show("FACIAL CARE - all under $25", cats_kw=['FACIAL CARE'], max_p=25, limit=60)
show("BATH/SHOWER preparations", cats_kw=['BATH/SHOWER'], max_p=20, limit=30)
show("HAND/BODY LOTION nice", cats_kw=['LOTION'], max_p=15, limit=30)
show("OILS body/facial natural", cats_kw=['OILS,ESSENTIAL'], max_p=20, limit=20)
show("Face masks", desc_kw=['MASK','MSK'], cats_kw=['FACIAL'], max_p=20, limit=20)
EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:48]:48}| {b[:30]}")

show("WELEDA skincare", brand_kw=['WELEDA'], max_p=25, limit=30)
show("ANDALOU / ACURE / PACIFICA", brand_kw=['ANDALOU','ACURE','PACIFICA','MYCHELLE','DERMA E','AVALON','ALBA'], max_p=25, limit=40)
show("LUMINARY / BETTER PLANET / HONEST / BURT", brand_kw=['LUMINARY','BETTER PLANET','HONEST','CLOROX COMPANY'], cats_kw=['FACIAL','LOTION','BATH'], max_p=15, limit=40)
show("SUNSCREEN / SPF", desc_kw=['SUNSCRN','SUNSCREEN','SPF'], cats_kw=['SUN CARE','FACIAL'], max_p=20, limit=20)
EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)} hits, showing {min(limit,len(out))}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:50]:50}| {b[:28]} | {c[:22]}")

show("ALOE VERA", desc_kw=['ALOE'], limit=40, max_p=25)
show("RAW HONEY", desc_kw=['RAW HONEY','RAW HNY','HONEY,RAW','HONEY,OG'], limit=20, max_p=40)
show("MANUKA HONEY", desc_kw=['MANUKA','MNKA'], limit=10, max_p=40)
show("APPLE CIDER VINEGAR", desc_kw=['APPLE CIDER','ACV','CIDER VINEGAR'], limit=15, max_p=30)
show("COCONUT OIL", desc_kw=['COCONUT OIL','CNUT OIL','CCNT OIL','COCO OIL'], limit=15, max_p=30)
show("HEMP / CHIA / FLAX seeds raw", desc_kw=['CHIA SEED','HEMP HEART','FLAXSEED','FLAX'], cats_kw=['NUTS,'], limit=15)
show("GHEE / CLARIFIED BUTTER", desc_kw=['GHEE'], limit=10, max_p=40)
show("MATCHA / SPIRULINA / CACAO", desc_kw=['MATCHA','SPIRULINA','CACAO','SUPERFOOD','TURMERIC'], limit=20, max_p=30)
show("ROSE WATER / FACIAL TONERS NATURAL", desc_kw=['ROSE WATER','ROSEWATER','TONER','WITCH HAZ'], cats_kw=['FACIAL'], limit=15, max_p=15)
show("BENTONITE CLAY / MASKS NATURAL", desc_kw=['CLAY','BENTONITE'], limit=10)
show("VITAMIN E OIL / JOJOBA OIL / ARGAN", desc_kw=['JOJOBA','ARGAN','VITAMIN E','VIT E','ROSEHIP'], cats_kw=['FACIAL','OILS'], limit=20, max_p=20)
show("EPSOM SALT", desc_kw=['EPSOM','MAGNESIUM FLK'], limit=10)
show("APRICOT / SWEET ALMOND OIL massage", cats_kw=['OILS,ESSENTIAL'], desc_kw=['ALMOND','APRICOT','GRAPESEED','JOJOBA','ARGAN','ROSEHIP'], limit=20)
EOF

    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

for b in sorted(brands):

Based on the search results, here's what I found about KIND bars shelf life and expiration dates:

## Shelf Life

KIND bars have a shelf life of around 6 to 12 months from the date of production, though some varieties may last longer or shorter. For specific varieties, some pressed KIND bars have a 12-month shelf life.

## Understanding Expiration Dates

KIND Bars come with a Best By date printed on the packaging, provided by the manufacturer as a guideline for when the product is expected to retain its optimal quality in terms of flavor and texture. The labeled "best by" dates are an estimate of optimal flavor and quality and not a date of expiration.

## Storage Tips to Maintain Freshness

To keep KIND bars fresh longer, store them in their original packaging in a cool, dry place, away from direct sunlight and any heat sources. High humidity can lead to moisture absorption, making the bars soggy and accelerating spoilage.

## Signs of Spoilage

Watch for stale taste or texture, an unusual or rancid odor (especially from the nuts), and visible mold, which all indicate the bar should not be consumed.

Based on the search results, here's what you need to know about Oatly shelf-stable oat milk shelf life and storage:

## Unopened Shelf-Stable Oat Milk

Oatly shelf-stable oat milk lasts about a year from the production date, whether you store it in the fridge or at room temperature. You can store a shelf-stable oat milk carton at room temperature before opening it.

## Once Opened

Once opened, both shelf-stable and refrigerated oat milk packages stay fresh for five to seven days if you treat them appropriately. The best way to store an opened shelf stable or refrigerated oat milk is to keep it cold in the fridge.

## Storage Tips

For best results with opened cartons, make sure to tightly reseal the container and store it in the refrigerator. The recommended storage temperature should be at or below 40 degrees Fahrenheit, ideally in the back of the fridge on the lower shelf.

Verified the items where bulk quantities could outrun their shelf life. Most are fine — flagging the three that need attention.

✅ Plenty of headroom (unopened years, no concern)
EVOO (Garcia 4 bottles) — 18–24 mo unopened, 6 mo opened. Open one at a time.
Pasta (12 boxes Rao's) — 2+ years dry pasta. Trivial.
Canned tomatoes / beans / soy sauce / mustard — 2–3 years.
Coconut oil, ACV, honey, epsom salt, clay — effectively indefinite.
Oats, granola, popcorn, chia/flax/hemp — 6–12 mo. Fine.
Pads / floss / toothpaste / deodorant / shampoo / bar soap — 2–5 years unopened. Stockpile away.
Sunscreen — 3 years (Thinkbaby).
Tea bags — 18–24 months.
KIND bars — Best-By is 6–12 mo from manufacture; arrive at WesShop with ~6 mo left. 24 bars = ~2/week → finished in 12 weeks. ✅
⚠️ Three items worth adjusting
1. McCormick Salsa (6 jars) — once opened, only 2–3 weeks fridge life. 6 jars × 2 wks = 12 wks of consecutive use. Doable but risky.

Suggest: cut to 3 jars (saves ~$8) — open one every 3 weeks, finishes by semester end. Frees ~$8.

2. Bove's Marinara (6 jars) — Rao's-style marinara only lasts 10–12 days fridge once opened. 6 jars × 10 days = 60 days of pasta nights, which works for a semester only if you eat pasta ≥2×/week.

OK to keep all 6 IF you're a regular pasta eater. Otherwise cut to 4. You can also transfer half a jar to the freezer (6 months) when you open one.

3. Oatly Shelf-Stable Oatmilk (6 cartons / 32 oz each) — unopened ~1 year, but only 5–7 days fridge once opened. Each carton = 4 cups. Drinking 1 cup/day = 4 days/carton (perfect), but if you only use a splash for coffee, 1 carton goes bad before you finish it.

Suggest: cut to 3 cartons if you use oatmilk only for coffee/cereal. Keep all 6 only if you drink it straight or do daily smoothies. Saves ~$10.

💡 Reclaimed budget (~$26) — what to add
If you trim those three, you free up $26 + the $24 already unspent = **$50 to redeploy**. Options:

Honey Pot or August organic tampons (if you want tampons too) — ~$5–8 per box
A second KIND-bar variety — $18.23 (24 more bars)
Hu Simple Milk Chocolate 6/2.1 oz — $22.96
Frontier Castor Oil + Sweet Almond Oil for hair/skin — ~$6
Wedderspoon Manuka Honey Drops (real manuka, retail $12+) — $6.61

import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:50]:50}| {b[:24]} | {c[:22]}")

show("VASELINE LOTION / PETROLEUM JELLY", desc_kw=['VASELINE','PETROLEUM','PETRLM','PETROLEM'], limit=20)

Bash Search remaining items: housewares, water, pens
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:50]:50}| {b[:24]} | {c[:22]}")

# Full housewares
show("ALL HOUSEWARES (fans, etc)", cats_kw=['HOUSEWARES'], limit=50)
show("ALL MISC NONFOODS", cats_kw=['MISC. NONFOODS'], limit=80)
show("PAPER PLASTIC HOUSEHOLD MISC", cats_kw=['PAPER, PLASTIC','SPECIALTY MISC'], limit=30)
# Petroleum jelly
show("JELLY / BALM / VASELINE alternatives", desc_kw=['BALM','JELLY','SALVE','OINTMENT'], cats_kw=['OINTMENTS','FACIAL','LOTION'], limit=20, max_p=15)
show("ALL OINTMENTS & MEDICINAL CREAMS", cats_kw=['OINTMENT'], limit=40)
# Distilled / spring water gallon
show("Big water gallons", desc_kw=['GALLON','GA,','1 GA','GA '], cats_kw=['BEVERAGE, WATER','WATER'], limit=15)
# Kosher salt big bag
show("KOSHER / SEA SALT big", desc_kw=['SALT'], cats_kw=['BULK','SEASONING'], limit=30, max_p=30)
# Diamond Crystal kosher salt or Morton
show("MORTON / DIAMOND salt", brand_kw=['MORTON','DIAMOND CRYSTAL','REDMOND','REDMND'], desc_kw=['SALT'], limit=15)
# Body spray, eau de toilette
show("EAU DE / SCENT / SPRAY", desc_kw=['EAU DE','PARFUM','COLOGNE','SCENT','ROLLERBALL','ROLL-ON,PRFM'], limit=20)
# Shower cap / hair bonnet  - try different desc
show("HAIR CAP search v2", desc_kw=['CAP,','SLEEP','BONNT','BNNET','SATIN'], cats_kw=['PERSONAL','MISC','HOUSEWARES'], limit=15)
# AirPods (clearly not but check)
show("HEADPHONES / TECH", desc_kw=['HEADPHONE','EARBUD','AIRPOD','EARPHONE','CHARGE'], limit=5)
EOF

❌ Not in WesShop catalog

Based on the search results, here are the key dates for Wesleyan University dining services for the 2026 spring semester and summer dining:

**Spring Semester End:**
Spring semester meal swipes expire on May 16, 2026. Additionally, dining operates on a limited schedule from Saturday 5/16 through Wednesday 5/20, with no dining available on Thursday, May 21st as the campus prepares for Reunion Commencement activities.

**Summer Dining Start:**
Summer Dining 2026 begins on Tuesday, 5/26/26 and runs through 8/25/26. Fall meal plans begin on 8/26/26.

For summer dining hours, dining is available daily from 11am - 1pm and 5pm-7pm located in Summerfields.

This changes the math significantly — let me lay it out.

The hard deadline
Today: May 12, 2026
Your points expire: May 16, 2026 (4 days from now — last day to use any meals or points for the academic year)
Dining gap (campus closed for food): May 17 – May 25 (9 days, you're on your own)
Summer dining starts: May 26, 2026 at Summerfields (11am–1pm, 5pm–7pm)
⚠️ Your 969 points cannot be saved for summer
Summer dining is a completely separate paid plan — your spring points do not carry over. Summer options are:

50 swipes for $500 or 150 swipes for $1500 (apply through dining)
Walk-up: $15 brunch / $18 dinner (credit card) or $11/meal with Middletown Cash
So the real question isn't "how many do I need until summer" — it's "how do I split 969 points between WesShop preorder and on-campus meals over the next 4 days?"

Recommended split
If you eat on-campus normally (Usdan/Pi/Summerfields) at ~$10–12/meal for the next 5 dining days (5/12 – 5/16):

Use	Estimate
~3 meals/day × 5 days × $11/meal	~$165 in points for daily eating
WesShop bulk preorder	~$800
Buffer for last-day Pi/Weshop snack grabs	~$5
Total	$969
If you've been mostly cooking and don't eat on campus much:

Use	Estimate
1 meal/day × 5 days × $11	~$55
WesShop preorder	~$910
Buffer	~$5
Total	$970
What about the 9-day gap (5/17 – 5/25)?
You'll need outside money for those 9 days — either:

Eat from your bulk WesShop stash (one reason to load up now)
Middletown grocery runs (Stop & Shop, Trader Joe's, Aldi)
Restaurants on cash/credit
Budget guess: ~$50–100 of your own money for that stretch, depending on how much WesShop pantry you front-loaded
So the practical answer
Keep ~$55–165 in points for next-4-days dining (depends on how much you'll eat on campus)
Spend the rest (~$800–910) on WesShop bulk preorder
The gap and summer = your own money, not points

Bash Find regular table salt
IN
python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

# ALL salt - every match
print("=== EVERY salt item under $30 ===")
out=[]
for r in rows:
    pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
    d_u=(d or "").upper()
    if 'SALT' in d_u and co<=30:
        out.append(r)
out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:60]:
    print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:50]:50}| {b[:25]} | {c[:22]}")

print("\n=== Iodized / table salt specifically ===")
for r in rows:
    pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
    d_u=(d or "").upper()
    if any(k in d_u for k in ['IODIZED','TABLE SALT','REAL SALT','FINE SALT','GROUND SALT']):
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d}  | {b} | {c[:25]}")
EOF

Based on the search results, here's what I found about Schmidt's Natural Deodorant shelf life:

## Unopened Shelf Life

Schmidt's products have a shelf life of about 18 months unopened and roughly 12 months after being opened. Another source from Schmidt's customer service notes that their deodorants are freshest for about 18-24 months unopened, and 12 months once opened. Additionally, the product life can also increase if kept in a refrigerator.

## Finding the Expiration Date

You can find a shelf life symbol on the back of Schmidt's products, which indicates the number of months that a cosmetic is safe to use after opening.

## Storage Tips

To maximize shelf life, it's best to store your unopened deodorant in a cool place away from heat and direct sunlight, as exposure to warm temperatures can potentially damage the product.

2. Regular table salt (Morton-style iodized)?
No — WesShop only stocks sea salt. Closest options:

Badia Fine Sea Salt 6/10 oz — $9.99 (60 oz total, basically iodized-salt texture/cheapest)
Vigo Mediterranean (already on list)
For Nigerian stew, sea salt works identically — actually better since no anti-caking agents. Recommend swapping Vigo for Badia Fine if you want it to feel/pour like regular salt. Saves ~$22.

3. Beef stick swap — agreed, dropping
Frees $74.26. Options to redirect:

More lotion (your stated priority)

Tom's of Maine Floss ($20.56 for 6 floss packs)
4. Deodorant expiry — important
Per Schmidt's official FAQ:

Unopened: 18–24 months
Opened: 12 months
Your current plan of 8 sticks is too many. If you open one at a time and use each over ~2 months (typical), you'd be on stick #8 in month 16 — past its unopened shelf life by then. Sticks toward the end will go waxy/lose scent.

Recommendation: cut to 5 sticks = ~10 months of rotating use, all within shelf life. Saves $15.24.

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

# EVERY single item with SALT in description, no filter
print("=== EVERY salt item, all prices ===")
out=[]
for r in rows:
    pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
    d_u=(d or "").upper()
    if 'SALT' in d_u and not any(k in d_u for k in ['BATH SALT','BAR SOAP','BAR,SOAP','SP,','DEOD','LOTION','BODY WASH','CHOC','SOAK','BAR ','SHAMPOO','COND','CRACKER','PRTL','CHIP','SCRUB','LIP','HND','HAND','MOUTH','SHWR','SHOWER','BAG,SOAP','MOUSSE','POPCORN','CARMEL','CARAMEL','HAIR','SP,','SOAP','RICE','PASTA']):
        out.append(r)
out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:80]:
    print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:55]:55}| {b[:30]:30} | {c[:30]}")

# Every SEASONING/EXTRACTS item
print("\n=== ALL SEASONING/EXTRACTS items, sorted by price ===")
out=[]
for r in rows:
    pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
    if (c or "").upper().startswith('SEASONING') or (c or "").upper().startswith('SEASONINGS'):
        out.append(r)
out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
print(f"Total: {len(out)}")

# Look for ones with just "salt" word
print("\n=== Just shaker salt / cooking salt ===")
for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out:
    d_u=(d or "").upper()
    if 'SALT' in d_u and 'LESS' not in d_u and 'GARLIC SALT' not in d_u:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d}  | {b}")

# FANS — search more broadly. Maybe FOOD STORAGE, MISC ITEMS
print("\n=== Items priced between $35-$50 in HOUSEWARES / MISC. NONFOODS / PERSONAL CARE ===")
for r in rows:
    pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
    c_u=(c or "").upper()
    if 35 <= co <= 50 and any(k in c_u for k in ['HOUSEWARES','MISC. NONFOODS','PERSONAL CARE','PAPER, PLASTIC','SPECIALTY MISC']):
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:55]:55}| {b[:25]} | {c[:25]}")

# Search keyword "FAN"
print("\n=== Anything with 'FAN' in description ===")
for r in rows:
    pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
    if 'FAN' in (d or "").upper():
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d}  | {b}  | {c}")
EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
import openpyxl
wb = openpyxl.load_workbook('/Users/naomiivie/Downloads/Weshop ECHO Catalog 1.xlsx', data_only=True)
ws = wb['Updated 2026']
rows=[]
for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=12, values_only=True):
    _, pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost = row
    if pid is None or cost is None: continue
    rows.append((pid, brand, desc, cat, pack, cost))

def show(title, cats_kw=None, desc_kw=None, brand_kw=None, max_p=None, limit=60, exclude_kw=None):
    out=[]
    for r in rows:
        pid,b,d,c,p,co = r
        c=(c or "").upper(); d=(d or "").upper(); b=(b or "").upper()
        if cats_kw and not any(k in c for k in cats_kw): continue
        if desc_kw and not any(k in d for k in desc_kw): continue
        if brand_kw and not any(k in b for k in brand_kw): continue
        if exclude_kw and any(k in d for k in exclude_kw): continue
        if max_p and co>max_p: continue
        out.append(r)
    out.sort(key=lambda x:x[5])
    print(f"\n=== {title} ({len(out)}) ===")
    for pid,b,d,c,p,co in out[:limit]:
        print(f"  ${co:6.2f} [{p:>14}] {d[:55]:55}| {b[:28]} | {c[:25]}")

# Big bag rice (not pouches)
show("BULK RICE - big bags / dry rice", cats_kw=['BEANS GRAINS','BULK'], desc_kw=['JASMINE','BASMATI','RICE,LB','RICE LB','LB RICE','RICE,WHITE','BRN RICE','BROWN RICE','LB,','BULK,RICE','RICE,OG'], max_p=60, exclude_kw=['POUCH','RTE','RICE GRITS','RICE CAKE','PUFFED','MILK','PROTEIN','CHIA','SAU','RAMEN','CRACKER','CUP','BREAD','PILAF','BURGER','BOWL','MAC','BEER','VIN','MEAL','CKD'])
# All rice items, just by category
show("ALL RICE / GRAIN items in BEANS GRAINS & RICE", cats_kw=['BEANS GRAINS & RICE'], desc_kw=['RICE'], limit=60)
# Frozen potatoes
show("FROZEN POTATOES", cats_kw=['FROZEN FRUITS','FZ APPETIZERS'], desc_kw=['POTATO','POT,','POTAT','HASHBROWN','HASH BR','TOTS','FRENCH FRI','FRIES','TATER'], limit=30)
# Any potato anywhere
show("Anything potato", desc_kw=['POTATO','HASHBR','TOTS','TATER','FRIES'], limit=30, max_p=60)
# Big pasta cases / bulk
show("PASTA case big variety", cats_kw=['PASTA, PACKAGED'], max_p=25, limit=40)
EOF

Based on the search results, here's what you need to know about the shelf life and storage of uncooked white rice, jasmine rice, and basmati rice:

## Shelf Life

White, jasmine and basmati rice all have an indefinite shelf life, when kept free from contaminants. More specifically, white rice (including jasmine and basmati) that has had its bran and germ layers removed stores virtually indefinitely under proper conditions, with the USDA and food storage experts estimating that properly sealed white rice remains safe and nutritious for 4 to 5 years at room temperature — and for up to 25 to 30 years in food-grade sealed containers with oxygen absorbers.

Some manufacturers like Mahatma Rice recommend that these rice products are best by two years (730 days) after the date of manufacture if stored properly and protected from infestation and extreme temperature fluctuations.

## Storage Guidelines

To maximize shelf life, follow these storage recommendations:

- Keep rice in securely sealed containers that keep out unwanted moisture and eliminate the risk of infestation, and once opened, store it in a tightly closed container that keeps out dust, moisture and other contaminants.
- Store uncooked rice in a dry and cool environment, and keep it far away from foods such as onions or garlic as rice will absorb strong aromas.
- For added protection against bugs, dust or other contaminants, uncooked jasmine rice may also be stored in the fridge or freezer in a sealed airtight container or resealable heavy-duty freezer bag.

## Codex · 2026-05-12T05:46:16.844Z · commentary

Got you. I’m going back to the workbook itself for `Vaseline`, `Morton`, and lotion terms, and I’ll verify lotion shelf life from product/dermatology sources so we don’t accidentally stockpile something that turns weird before you use it.

## Codex · 2026-05-12T05:46:50.072Z · final_answer

You were right about regular salt. I found it in the catalog:

**Kayco Table Salt**
Product `1040914` — `SALT,TABLE - 24/26 OZ` — **$37.68**
That is 24 cartons, 26 oz each = **39 lb of regular table salt**.

For **Morton** specifically: the catalog has **Morton & Bassett spices**, but not Morton table salt. If WesShop physically sells Morton, it is probably in-store inventory, not in this ECHO preorder sheet.

For **Vaseline lotion / Vaseline jelly**: I searched `VASELINE`, `PETROLEUM`, `PETR`, and related terms across the workbook. **No Vaseline brand and no petroleum jelly in the preorder catalog.** Physical WesShop may still have it, but it is not in this catalog.

Best lotion substitute in the preorder:
**Hain / Alba Botanica Very Emollient Body Lotion, 32 oz**
`BODY LTN,VERY EMOL,DRY / ORIGINAL / UNSCENTED / COCONUT` — **$11.20 per 32 oz bottle**
This is the closest “big daily body lotion” option. Alba says the Very Emollient lotion is hypoallergenic and has no petrolatum, so it is not Vaseline-like, but it is a good bulk lotion.

Shelf life: lotions usually last best **1-3 years unopened**, but once opened I would treat each bottle as **use within 12 months**. FDA says cosmetic shelf life depends on formula/storage and manufacturers are responsible for determining it, so check the bottle date/PAO symbol when it arrives. Store extras cool/dark, not by a window or heater.

**Updated direction**
Keep the fan as **in-store purchase**, not preorder. I would reserve **$40 of the $100** for the fan, leaving about **$60 for food/meals**.

Add:
- Kayco Table Salt, 1 case: $37.68
- Multiple rice cases: white jasmine/basmati rice is fine to stockpile. Uncooked white rice lasts years if kept dry/sealed.
- Multiple pasta cases: dry pasta also lasts years.
- Potato cases only if you have freezer space. They are frozen, so don’t overdo it if freezer space is small.

Sources: [FDA cosmetics shelf life](https://www.fda.gov/cosmetics/cosmetics-labeling/shelf-life-and-expiration-dating-cosmetics), [Alba Botanica Very Emollient lotion](https://www.albabotanica.com/products-alba/very-emollient-body-lotion-4-2/), [Mahatma rice storage FAQ](https://mahatmarice.com/faqs/storage/)

## Codex · 2026-05-12T06:07:35.811Z · final_answer

Yes exactly. **Morton & Bassett Spices is not Morton Salt.**

It’s a separate spice brand in the catalog. So when the sheet says things like:

`MORTON & BASSETT SPICES — SEASONING...`

that means fancy spices/seasonings, not the Morton table salt brand with the blue canister.

The actual regular table salt I found is:

`KAYCO DRY BAYONNE — SALT,TABLE - 24/26 OZ — $37.68`

So: **Morton brand salt = not in preorder catalog. Regular table salt = yes, Kayco.**

this should have salt noMorton & Bassett spices,

## Codex · 2026-05-12T06:07:56.380Z · final_answer

I checked that brand specifically: **Morton & Bassett Spices does not have plain salt in this catalog.** It has spices like cayenne, cumin, garlic powder, curry, paprika, rosemary, vanilla extract, etc.

The confusing part is that the category is called `SEASONING/EXTRACTS/PKGD SALT`, so Morton & Bassett appears inside a “salt” category, but the individual product descriptions are not salt.

For actual salt, the strongest matches are:

- **Kayco Table Salt** — `SALT,TABLE - 24/26 OZ` — **$37.68**
- **Badia Fine Sea Salt** — `SEA SALT,FINE - 6/10 OZ` — **$9.99**
- **Badia Kosher Salt** — `SALT,KOSHER - 12/8 OZ` — **$24.03**
- **Cargill Kosher Flakes** — `SALT,KOSHER FLAKES,BOX - 9/3 LB` — **$69.86**

For “regular salt,” get **Kayco Table Salt**.

## Codex · 2026-05-12T06:13:25.733Z · final_answer

Not true perfume/body spray, sadly. I re-searched broadly and the catalog has **no actual perfume, body spray, body mist, cologne, or fragrance spray** in the normal sense.

Closest things it does have:

- **Frontier Aroma Mists** — eucalyptus, ginger/mint, peppermint, lavender, tangerine/grapefruit — `1/4 FZ`, **$5.92 each**
  More aromatherapy/room or pillow mist than body spray.

- **Frontier Essential Oil Mists** — “Chill Pill,” “Panic Button,” workout refresh/purify/motivate — **$3.72-$4.57 each**
  Again, more aromatherapy mist.

- **Pillow Potion Mist** — **$4.57**
  Not body spray.

- **Weleda Aroma Body Washes** — scented shower gels, **$6.88**
  Not leave-on fragrance.

- **Beard cologne oils** — bourbon / glacial / wood pine, **$11.75**
  Very masc-coded, not body spray.

So if you want “I smell good all day” stuff, buy that physically at WesShop/Target. From preorder, the best substitute is maybe **Frontier Aroma Mist Tangerine/Grapefruit** or **Lavender**, but I would not spend bulk points on it as perfume.
